Do not rebuild or modify the application binaries. Configure each runtime using the environment variables below.\n4: \n5: ## 1. API Service (`api_image` on ECS Fargate)\n6: \n7: - **Binary**: `/usr/local/bin/clearledger-api`\n8: - **Listening port**: `8080` (`PORT=8080`)\n9: - **Required environment variables**:\n10:   - `PORT`: `\"8080\"`\n11:   - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n12:   - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json` (`us-east-1`)\n13:   - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n14:   - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n15:   - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: control-plane endpoint reachable from the container (`http://aws:4566`)\n16:   - `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n17:   - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n18:   - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n19:   - `VALKEY_URL`: Valkey connection URL `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n20:   - `CACHE_TTL_SECONDS`: `\"90\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"90\"`)\n21:   - `AUTH_ISSUER`: Cognito issuer URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`)\n22:   - `AUTH_AUDIENCES`: comma-separated Cognito client IDs (`<read_client_id>,<write_client_id>,<admin_client_id>`)\n23:   - `AUTH_JWKS_URL`: Cognito JWKS URL (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>/.well-known/jwks.json`)\n24:   - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the API CloudWatch Log Group\n25:   - `SERVICE_INSTANCE_ID` (optional): instance identifier returned in `X-ClearLedger-Instance` (defaults to `HOSTNAME`)\n26: \n27: Upon startup, `clearledger-api` connects to PostgreSQL and serves HTTP traffic on `0.0.0.0:8080`. The application binaries do **not** run database migrations automatically: `deploy.sh` must idempotently apply the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`, `clearledger.idempotency_keys`), all required relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, all state-transition, cross-table, and append-only triggers across all four tables, and its three indexes as defined in `services/rds.md` before `GET /health/ready` reports `200 OK` (`checks.postgres = \"UP\"`). During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n28: \n29: ## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n30: \n31: - **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n32: - **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n33: - **Required environment variables**:\n34:   - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n35:   - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n36:   - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n37:   - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n38:   - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n39:   - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n40:   - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n41:   - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n42: \n43: For each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n44: - Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n45: - Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n46: - Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n47: - If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n48: \n49: ## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n50: \n51: - **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n52: - **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n53: - **Required environment variables**:\n54:   - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n55:   - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n56:   - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n57:   - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n58:   - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n59:   - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n60:   - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n61:   - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n62:   - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n63: \n64: Queries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n65: \n66: ## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n67: \n68: - **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n69: - **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n70: - **Required environment variables**:\n71:   - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n72:   - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n73:   - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n74:   - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n75:   - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n76:   - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n77:   - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n78:   - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n79:   - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n80:   - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n81: \n82: Selects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n83: \n84: ## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n85: \n86: All protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n87: \n88: - `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n89: - `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n90: - `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n91: \n92: Missing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n93: \n94: ## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n95: \n96: - **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n97: - **Bidirectional Multi-Store Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n98:   - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n99:   - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane and all derived data stores against PostgreSQL before exiting `0`:\n100:     - All unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) must be relayed to SQS.\n101:     - The DynamoDB projection table must contain **only** partitions for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements`, and each partition `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` must contain **only** `SK = \"STATE\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#00000001\" .. \"EVENT#<version:08d>\"` with every attribute (including `AccountIndex` `GSI1PK` / `GSI1SK`, `updated_at`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, and each event's `entry_id`, `memo`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, and `envelope`) matching PostgreSQL.\n102:     - Valkey must contain **only** `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` keys for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements` (purging any orphan settlement keys as well as any stray keys outside the `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` namespace), and every settlement in `clearledger.settlements` must be actively populated in Valkey at `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` with a valid TTL in `(0, 90]` and a canonical `SettlementProjection` JSON payload matching the authoritative projection state (so `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` immediately after `deploy.sh` hits cache and returns `X-ClearLedger-Source: cache`).\n103:     - The S3 audit archive bucket (`s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>`) must contain objects **only** under `ledger-audit/`, every object key and body must conform to `ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson` with a contiguous, gap-free slice of committed `clearledger.outbox` records in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq` and strictly disjoint, non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals across batch objects, the union of all S3 batch records must match `clearledger.outbox` **1-to-1** (every committed outbox event present in S3 **exactly once** with its exact canonical JSON `payload`, and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL), and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) must be permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each canonical batch remains.\n104:     - Any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu (including deleting any detached out-of-band `<resource_prefix>`-prefixed customer-managed IAM policies via `DeletePolicy`), as well as any outbound egress rules on the `rds` or `valkey` security groups (`egress = []`) and any unrestricted `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` egress rules on the `alb` security group, must be removed during `deploy.sh` convergence.\n105: - **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n106:   - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including customer-managed KMS keys scheduled for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n107: - **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n108: \n\n(End of file - During `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`, `clearledger-api` maps PostgreSQL constraint and trigger exceptions (`SQLSTATE 23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001`) to HTTP `400 Bad Request` (`invalid_settlement`).\n\n## 2. Projector Worker (`projector_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: SQS event source mapping from the main SQS queue (`batch_size = 5`, `maximum_batching_window_in_seconds = 0`, `function_response_types = [\"ReportBatchItemFailures\"]`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `PROJECTION_TABLE`: name of the DynamoDB projection table\n  - `VALKEY_URL`: `redis://<valkey_host>:<valkey_port>`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the projector CloudWatch Log Group\n\nFor each valid event envelope in the SQS batch:\n- Inserts the event item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"EVENT#<8-digit-zero-padded-version>\"`) in DynamoDB using a conditional `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` write.\n- Updates the aggregate state item (`PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`, `SK = \"STATE\"`, `GSI1PK = \"ACCOUNT#<account_id>\"`, `GSI1SK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"`) using a conditional write only when `aggregateVersion` is strictly greater than the stored `version`. Duplicate or older versions are ignored idempotently; if an older version (such as `v1`) arrives after a newer version, any missing static metadata (`reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`) is backfilled without regressing `version`, `status`, or `clearing_stage`.\n- Deletes the Valkey key `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`.\n- If any record fails validation or deserialization, its `messageId` is returned in `batchItemFailures` so SQS retries only the failed message and routes poison messages to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4` receives.\n\n## 3. Outbox Relay Worker (`relay_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 1 minute (`rate(1 minute)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `SQS_QUEUE_URL`: URL of the main SQS event queue\n  - `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE`: `\"50\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"50\"`)\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the relay CloudWatch Log Group\n\nQueries up to `OUTBOX_BATCH_SIZE` rows from `clearledger.outbox` where `published_at IS NULL` ordered by `seq ASC`, sends each event envelope to `SQS_QUEUE_URL`, and updates `published_at = NOW(), attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = NULL`.\n\n## 4. Audit Archiver Worker (`archiver_image` on AWS Lambda)\n\n- **Bootstrap**: `/var/runtime/bootstrap`\n- **Trigger**: EventBridge Scheduler every 5 minutes (`rate(5 minutes)`)\n- **Required environment variables**:\n  - `AWS_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`: region from `config.json`\n  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`: `\"test\"`\n  - `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`: `\"http://aws:4566\"`\n  - `DATABASE_URL`: `postgres://<db_username>:<db_password>@<rds_host>:<rds_port>/<db_name>`\n  - `AUDIT_BUCKET`: name of the S3 audit archive bucket\n  - `AUDIT_PREFIX`: `\"ledger-audit/\"` (must be explicitly set to `\"ledger-audit/\"`)\n  - `AUDIT_BATCH_SIZE` (optional): defaults to `100`\n  - `CLOUDWATCH_LOG_GROUP`: name of the archiver CloudWatch Log Group\n\nSelects published outbox rows (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) ordered by `seq ASC`, writes a newline-delimited JSON object to `s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, and sets `archived_at = NOW()`.\n\n## 5. OAuth2 Scopes and Authorization\n\nAll protected endpoints validate Bearer JWTs issued by the Cognito User Pool against `AUTH_ISSUER`, `AUTH_JWKS_URL`, and `AUTH_AUDIENCES`. Scopes are strictly non-hierarchical:\n\n- `clearledger/read`: permitted only on `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` and `GET /v1/settlements/{id}/ledger`.\n- `clearledger/write`: permitted only on `POST /v1/settlements` and `POST /v1/settlements/{id}/entries`.\n- `clearledger/admin`: permitted only on `POST /v1/admin/projections/{id}/rebuild`.\n\nMissing or invalid tokens return `401 Unauthorized`; valid tokens lacking the exact endpoint scope return `403 Forbidden`.\n\n## 6. Recovery Drills, Bidirectional Data-Plane Reconciliation, and Lifecycle\n\n- **Backlog & DLQ**: When the projector event source mapping is disabled, API writes commit to PostgreSQL and enqueue on SQS while `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` returns `404` until re-enabled. Duplicate, out-of-order, and concurrent event deliveries are handled without version regression; invalid envelopes are retried and routed to the DLQ after `maxReceiveCount = 4`.\n- **Bidirectional Multi-Store Convergence in `deploy.sh`**:\n  - PostgreSQL (`clearledger.settlements`, `clearledger.events`, `clearledger.outbox`) is the authoritative system of record; DynamoDB, Valkey, and S3 are derived read/archive stores.\n  - Because `clearledger-projector` writes `EVENT#*` items conditionally with `attribute_not_exists(PK) AND attribute_not_exists(SK)` and updates `STATE` conditionally only when `aggregateVersion > current_version`, and because `clearledger-audit-archiver` only archives rows where `published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`, `deploy.sh` must converge both the control plane and all derived data stores against PostgreSQL before exiting `0`:\n    - All unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) must be relayed to SQS.\n    - The DynamoDB projection table must contain **only** partitions for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements`, and each partition `PK = \"SETTLEMENT#<settlement_id>\"` must contain **only** `SK = \"STATE\"` and `SK = \"EVENT#00000001\" .. \"EVENT#<version:08d>\"` with every attribute (including `AccountIndex` `GSI1PK` / `GSI1SK`, `updated_at`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, and each event's `entry_id`, `memo`, `occurred_at`, `correlation_id`, and `envelope`) matching PostgreSQL.\n    - Valkey must contain **only** `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` keys for settlements that exist in `clearledger.settlements` (purging any orphan settlement keys as well as any stray keys outside the `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` namespace), and every settlement in `clearledger.settlements` must be actively populated in Valkey at `clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>` with a valid TTL in `(0, 90]` and a canonical `SettlementProjection` JSON payload matching the authoritative projection state (so `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` immediately after `deploy.sh` hits cache and returns `X-ClearLedger-Source: cache`).\n    - The S3 audit archive bucket (`s3://<AUDIT_BUCKET>`) must contain objects **only** under `ledger-audit/`, every object key and body must conform to `ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson` with a contiguous, gap-free slice of committed `clearledger.outbox` records in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq` and strictly disjoint, non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals across batch objects, the union of all S3 batch records must match `clearledger.outbox` **1-to-1** (every committed outbox event present in S3 **exactly once** with its exact canonical JSON `payload`, and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL), and all noncurrent object `Versions` (`IsLatest = false`) and `DeleteMarkers` (`ListObjectVersions` / `DeleteObject` with `VersionId`) must be permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each canonical batch remains.\n    - Any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu (including deleting any detached out-of-band `<resource_prefix>`-prefixed customer-managed IAM policies via `DeletePolicy`), as well as any outbound egress rules on the `rds` or `valkey` security groups (`egress = []`) and any unrestricted `0.0.0.0/0` or `::/0` egress rules on the `alb` security group, must be removed during `deploy.sh` convergence.\n- **Prefix-Scoped Teardown in `destroy.sh`**:\n  - `destroy.sh` must cleanly tear down all Terraform-managed resources as well as any out-of-band operational resources or policy attachments/versions scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (by name prefix or `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` tag, including customer-managed KMS keys scheduled for deletion) while keeping all `cl-base-*` baseline resources intact.\n- **Structured Logs**: All four workloads emit JSON log lines containing `correlationId` (propagated from `X-Correlation-Id`) to their configured CloudWatch Log Groups without leaking `db_password` or Cognito `client_secret` values.\n","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":108,"totalLines":108,"truncated":false}},"title":"workspace/contracts/runtime.md","time":{"start":1791610420653,"end":1791610420681}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_08e3e70e01d550df016ac9ce34901c87d19a0edbeebd828921"}},"id":"prt_1244d7da9001E5QIsE7w1cdL2t","sessionID":"ses_edbb2a5faffeLpxH18bEEnSF2Y","messageID":"msg_1244d71b400100T8R9m1fSTfMO"}}

Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n9: - **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n10: - **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n11: - **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy, and any detached out-of-band customer-managed IAM policies prefixed with `<resource_prefix>` must also be deleted (`DeletePolicy`, after deleting any non-default policy versions).\n12: \n13: ## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n14: \n15: 1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n16:    - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n17:    - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n18:    - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n19: 2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n20:    - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n21:    - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n22:      - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n23:      - Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime. No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, and `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys.\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy drift reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies (`DeleteRolePolicy`) or attached customer-managed/AWS-managed policies (`DetachRolePolicy`) added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy, and any detached out-of-band customer-managed IAM policies prefixed with `<resource_prefix>` must also be deleted (`DeletePolicy`, after deleting any non-default policy versions).\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - Provision a DB subnet group (`aws_db_subnet_group`) spanning strictly the private subnets (`network.private_subnet_ids`).\n4: - Provision a single PostgreSQL 16 RDS instance (`aws_db_instance`) with:\n5:   - `engine = \"postgres\"`\n6:   - `engine_version = \"16.3\"` (or `16.*`)\n7:   - `instance_class = \"db.t4g.micro\"`\n8:   - `db_name`, `username`, and `password` from `/workspace/config/config.json`\n9:   - `vpc_security_group_ids` containing the `rds` security group\n14: - Re-running `deploy.sh` must never replace the RDS instance or lose committed data.\n15: \n16: ## Required Database Schema, Relational Invariants, and Triggers\n17: \n18: The pre-built Rust binaries (`clearledger-api`, `clearledger-outbox-relay`, and `clearledger-audit-archiver`) do **not** run database migrations on startup. `clearledger-api` relies on PostgreSQL `CHECK`/`UNIQUE`/`FOREIGN KEY` constraints and PL/pgSQL triggers to enforce domain, schema, and state-transition invariants at the database layer (mapping SQLSTATE `23514`, `23503`, `23502`, `23505`, and `P0001` exceptions to HTTP `400 Bad Request`), and `GET /health/ready` verifies that all four tables, all three indexes, and user-defined triggers on all four tables exist in the `clearledger` schema before reporting `checks.postgres = \"UP\"`.\n19: \n20: `deploy.sh` must idempotently initialize the `clearledger` schema, all four tables, their constraints, their triggers, and all three indexes on the RDS PostgreSQL instance.\n21: \n22: ### 1. Table `clearledger.settlements`\n23: \n24: - **Columns**:\n25:   - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n26:   - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n27:   - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n28:   - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n29:   - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n30:   - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n31:   - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n32:   - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n33:   - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n34:   - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n35:   - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n36:   - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n37:   - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n38: - **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n39:   - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n40:   - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n41:   - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n42:     - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n43:     - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n44:     - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n45:     - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n46: \n47: ### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n48: \n49: - **Columns**:\n50:   - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n51:   - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n52:   - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n53:   - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n54:   - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n55:   - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n56:   - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n57:   - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n58:   - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n59:   - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n60: - **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n61:   - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n62:   - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n63:   - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); Table `clearledger.settlements`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `account_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `reference TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `debit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `credit_party TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_status TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `current_stage TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `last_entry_id UUID NULL`\n  - `last_memo TEXT NULL`\n  - `version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `entry_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n  - `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger)**:\n  - Trimmed string length bounds and allowed status values must match `CreateSettlementRequest` and `AppendEntryRequest` in `openapi.yaml` (including `1..256` trimmed characters when `last_memo` is non-null), with trimmed `debit_party <> credit_party`, `version >= 1`, and monotonic timestamps (`updated_at >= created_at` on insert and `NEW.updated_at >= OLD.updated_at` on update).\n  - At initiation (`version = 1`), `entry_count = 0`, `current_status = 'INITIATED'`, `last_entry_id IS NULL`, and `updated_at = created_at`. For `version > 1`, `entry_count = version - 1`, `current_status <> 'INITIATED'`, and `last_entry_id IS NOT NULL`.\n  - On `UPDATE`: header columns (`settlement_id`, `account_id`, `reference`, `debit_party`, `credit_party`, `created_at`) are immutable; each update must step `version` and `entry_count` by `+1` with a new `last_entry_id` (`NEW.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id`); and `current_status` must follow the clearing lifecycle progression:\n    - The non-`DISPUTED` clearing status rank order is `INITIATED` (`0`) < `VALIDATED` (`1`) < `RESERVED` (`2`) < `CLEARED` (`3`) < `SETTLED` (`4`) < `RECONCILED` (`5`).\n    - `RECONCILED` is strictly terminal: once `OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`, any `UPDATE` on the settlement row must be rejected (even if `NEW.current_status = 'RECONCILED'`).\n    - From any non-`RECONCILED`, non-`DISPUTED` status (`INITIATED`, `VALIDATED`, `RESERVED`, `CLEARED`, `SETTLED`), `NEW.current_status` may transition to `'DISPUTED'` or to any status whose rank is monotonic non-decreasing (`rank(NEW.current_status) >= rank(OLD.current_status)` — permitting same-status updates such as `CLEARED` -> `CLEARED` and single- or multi-stage forward advances such as `INITIATED` -> `VALIDATED` or `INITIATED` -> `CLEARED`, while rejecting any backward rank regression where `rank(NEW.current_status) < rank(OLD.current_status)` such as `CLEARED` -> `RESERVED`).\n    - From `OLD.current_status = 'DISPUTED'`, `NEW.current_status` may only remain `'DISPUTED'` or resolve to terminal `'RECONCILED'`.\n\n### 2. Table `clearledger.events`\n\n- **Columns**:\n  - `seq BIGSERIAL NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY`\n  - `event_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE`\n  - `settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES clearledger.settlements(settlement_id) ON DELETE CASCADE`\n  - `aggregate_version INTEGER NOT NULL`\n  - `event_type TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `correlation_id TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL`\n  - `occurred_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL`\n  - `payload JSONB NOT NULL`\n  - `created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()`\n- **Required invariants (enforced via `UNIQUE`/`CHECK` constraints and `BEFORE INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` triggers)**:\n  - Composite `UNIQUE (settlement_id, aggregate_version)` and `UNIQUE (settlement_id, idempotency_key)`, with trimmed length bounds on `correlation_id` and `idempotency_key` matching `openapi.yaml`.\n  - `payload` must conform strictly to `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` in `schemas/events.schema.json` (including `additionalProperties: false` on both the envelope and `payload.data`, required non-null `accountId`, `reference`, `debitParty`, `creditParty`, `status`, and `clearingStage` in `payload.data` with trimmed string length bounds matching `openapi.yaml` and `1..256` trimmed characters when `data.memo` is non-null, column-to-envelope equality across `event_id`, `settlement_id`, `aggregate_version`, `event_type`, `correlation_id`, `idempotency_key`, and `occurred_at` compared as `TIMESTAMPTZ`, and version/kind/status/entryId coupling for `SettlementInitiated` at `v = 1` vs `LedgerEntryRecorded` at `v >= 2`).\n  - On `INSERT`: per-settlement `aggregate_version` must be contiguous starting at `1`; `data.entryId` must be unique per settlement across all `LedgerEntryRecorded` events; the event must match the parent row in `clearledger.settlements` (including header fields, `current_status`, `current_stage`, `last_entry_id`, `last_memo`, `version`, and `occurred_at = updated_at`, plus `occurred_at = created_at` at `v = 1`); 'NoSuchBucket', 'QueueDoesNotExist', 'AWS.SimpleQueueService.NonExistentQueue',\n+                                              'NotFoundException', 'NotFound', '404'):\n+            raise\n+\n+\n+def scoped(name, tags=()):\n+    # Baseline resources are never eligible, even if accidentally retagged.\n+    if name.startswith('cl-base-') or '/cl-base-' in name:\n+        return False\n+    if isinstance(tags, dict):\n+        tagged = tags.get('ClearLedgerDeployment') == PREFIX\n+    else:\n+        tagged = any(t.get('Key') == 'ClearLedgerDeployment' and t.get('Value') == PREFIX for t in iam.delete_policy(PolicyArn=arn)\n+\n+\n+def clean_iam(destroy=False):\n+    iam = client('iam')\n+    canonical_roles = {f'{PREFIX}-{r}' for r in ('ecs_execution', 'ecs_task', 'projector', 'relay', audit_valid(s3, bucket, rows):\n+    by_seq = {r['seq']: r for r in rows}\n+    seen = set()\n+    for obj in pages(s3, 'list_objects_v2', 'Contents', Bucket=bucket):\n+        match = re.fullmatch(r'ledger-audit/batch-(\\d{8,})-(\\d{8,})-([0-9a-f]{16})\\.ndjson', obj['Key'])\n+        if not match:\n+            return False\n+        first, last, digest = int(match[1]), int(match[2]), match[3]\n+        raw = s3.get_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=obj['Key'])['Body'].read()\n+        if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()[:16] != digest:\n+            return False\n+        seqs = [seq for seq in by_seq if first <= seq <= last]\n+        if not seqs or seqs[0] != first or seqs[-1] != last or seen.intersection(seqs):\n+            return False\n+        try:\n+        return False\n+        seen.update(seqs)\n+    return seen == set(by_seq)\n+\n+\n+def reconcile_audit(m, db):\n+    s3 = client('s3')\n+    bucket = m['audit']['bucket_name']\n+    rows = Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function connected to the main SQS queue via an event source mapping (`batch_size = 5`, `ReportBatchItemFailures` enabled) backed by a dead-letter queue (`maxReceiveCount = 4`). 3. **Outbox relay (`relay_image`)**: Polls **Amazon RDS for PostgreSQL** for committed outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`), publishes them to the main SQS queue, and stamps `published_at`. Deploy this as a container-based **AWS Lambda** function triggered every minute (`rate(1 minute)`) by **EventBridge Scheduler**. 4. **Audit archiver (`archiver_image`)**: Reads published settlement events from PostgreSQL (`published_at IS NOT NULL AND archived_at IS NULL`) and writes deterministic NDJSON audit batches (`ledger-audit/batch-*.ndjson`) to a private, versioned **Amazon S3** bucket. `services/sqs.md`, `services/lambda.md`, and `services/scheduler.md`: Main SQS queue and DLQ parameters, Lambda container workers, SQS event source mapping, and EventBridge Scheduler rules.   - `services/dynamodb.md`, `services/elasticache-valkey.md`, and `services/s3.md`: DynamoDB key schema (`PK`/`SK`), `AccountIndex` GSI (`GSI1PK`/`GSI1SK`), and point-in-time recovery, ElastiCache Valkey 8 (`cache.t4g.micro`), and S3 bucket versioning, KMS encryption, public-access block, and canonical batch key layout.   - `services/cognito.md`, `services/iam.md`, `services/kms.md`, and `services/cloudwatch-logs.md`: Cognito User Pool, resource server, and three client-credentials app clients, the six dedicated IAM roles (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`) with both least-privilege `Allow` statements and explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` guardrails, the four customer-managed KMS keys and aliases (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and the four CloudWatch Log Groups (`retention_in_days >= 14`). - `/workspace/contracts/openapi.yaml`: HTTP endpoints, request and response bodies, required request headers (`Idempotency-Key`, `X-Correlation-Id`), and response headers (`X-ClearLedger-Source`, `X-ClearLedger-Version`, `X-ClearLedger-Instance`). - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/events.schema.json`: Domain event envelope schema used on SQS and in S3 NDJSON audit batches. - `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`: JSON Schema for `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (`additionalProperties: false` throughout).  Deployment inputs (`resource_prefix`, `region`, `aws_endpoint_url`, `db_name`, `db_username`, `db_password`, and the four container image tags and digests: `api_image` / `clearledger/api:1.0.0`, `projector_image` / `clearledger/projector:1.0.0`, `relay_image` / `clearledger/relay:1.0.0`, `archiver_image` / `clearledger/archiver:1.0.0`) are stored in `/workspace/config/config.json`. All required cloud resources and IAM policies must be declared here and tracked in `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`. Do not create scored cloud resources via imperative `aws` CLI commands in `deploy.sh`. - `deploy.sh` (timeout: 720 seconds, max combined stdout/stderr: 8 MiB):   - Initializes and applies `/workspace/submission/infra` with local state at `/workspace/submission/infra/terraform.tfstate`.   - Idempotently creates the `clearledger` PostgreSQL schema, tables, relational `CHECK` / `UNIQUE` / `FOREIGN KEY` constraints, triggers, and indexes on the RDS instance as specified in `/workspace/contracts/services/rds.md`.   - Exports `/workspace/submission/manifest.json` (max 1 MiB) from your Terraform/OpenTofu outputs matching `/workspace/contracts/schemas/manifest.schema.json`.   - Waits until `GET <service_url>/health/ready` returns HTTP `200`.   - Handles repair and re-apply cleanly: re-running `deploy.sh` must keep the existing RDS instance, DynamoDB table, and S3 bucket intact without losing committed data, fix out-of-band control-plane drift (across SQS/DLQ attributes, EventBridge schedules, CloudWatch Log Group retention, Lambda environment variables, out-of-band inline or attached IAM policies on the six workload roles including deleting detached `<resource_prefix>` customer-managed policies, and security group egress rules), recreate and re-wire any deleted resources (such as the SQS queues, schedules, or the projector event source mapping), drain unpublished outbox rows (`published_at IS NULL`) through `outbox_relay`, reconcile DynamoDB (`STATE`, `EVENT#*`, and `AccountIndex` GSI attributes) and actively populate Valkey cache entries (`clearledger:settlement:<settlement_id>`) 1-to-1 against `clearledger.settlements` and `clearledger.events` (deleting any orphan partitions or stray items/keys), and reconcile the versioned S3 `ledger-audit/` archive 1-to-1 against `clearledger.outbox` with contiguous, non-overlapping sequence batches through `audit_archiver` (including purging noncurrent object `Versions` and `DeleteMarkers`) before exiting `0`. - `destroy.sh` (timeout: 900 seconds, max combined stdout/stderr: 8 MiB):   - Tears down all resources created for the active `resource_prefix` while leaving pre-existing baseline (`cl-base-*`) resources untouched.   - Cleans up any out-of-band operational resources or attachments scoped to `<resource_prefix>` (including inline or multi-version customer-managed IAM policies, breakglass IAM roles, non-empty versioned S3 buckets, DynamoDB tables, SQS queues, EventBridge schedules, customer-managed KMS keys/aliases, and `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>` CloudWatch Log Groups) so `terraform.tfstate` has zero remaining managed resources and post-destroy cloud inventory matches the pre-deployment baseline.
